### Assistant

_source line 400 · 2026-05-18T10:05:42.948Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The progress bar is stuck at 0% because the auto batch size detection takes a while, and the `\r` carriage return means the progress updates overwrite the same line. But `tail -1` reads the last physical line, which is still the 0% line because subsequent updates use `\r` and stay on the same line.

The issue is that the log uses `\r` (carriage return) for progress bar updates, which means the entire progress bar is a single line that gets overwritten. `tail -1` will keep showing the same content because it's the last line, and the progress bar updates just modify the beginning of that same line.


Instead of relying on `tail -1`, I should monitor the file size to detect if the process is still running and making progress, or look for completion markers in the log.
```

</details>
